================================================================================
Register Allocation Benefit Report  (66 test(s))
================================================================================

Legend (per the original log):
    peakLive        = peak simultaneously-live physical regs (CFG dataflow)
    maxIdxTouched   = max(reg.idx + reg.num); minimum legal amdhsa_next_free_*
    declared        = amdhsa_next_free_* read from the kernel descriptor
    metadataFix     = declared - maxIdxTouched   (free win, no codegen change)
    raGain          = maxIdxTouched - peakLive   (saving a real RA could give)
    potentialSaving = declared - peakLive        (overall headroom)
